## Overhead Candid City Stairs Romance

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "task_type": "photoreal_candid_city_stairs_romance",
      "language": "en",
      "priority": "highest",
      "version": "v1.0_OVERHEAD_STAIRS_HUG_FLOWERS_BEHIND_BACK"
    },
    "output": {
      "aspect_ratio": "3:4",
      "resolution": "high",
      "num_images": 4
    },
    "scene": {
      "concept": "a quiet romantic moment captured from above on a city staircase",
      "environment": "stone staircase with red-brown masonry wall, vintage iron railing, European urban setting, clean background with architectural texture",
      "composition": "elevated viewpoint looking down, couple centered on the stairs, railing partially in foreground for depth, strong leading lines from steps"
    },
    "subject": {
      "couple": "adult woman and adult man",
      "wardrobe": {
        "woman": "{argument name="woman's coat" default="beige trench coat"}, long dark hair, carrying a light neutral handbag",
        "man": "short-sleeve light shirt under a brown knit sweater vest, dark trousers"
      },
      "prop": "bouquet of {argument name="flower color" default="bright pink"} flowers wrapped in paper, held behind man’s back",
      "pose": "close hug, woman’s arms around his shoulders, calm intimate expression",
      "emotion": "soft, romantic, private moment"
    },
    "camera": {
      "shot": "telephoto candid from elevated angle",
      "lens_mm": 85,
      "aperture": 2.8,
      "focus": "sharp on couple and bouquet; background architecture slightly softened",
      "quality_tags": "ultra-photoreal, crisp textures, natural skin detail"
    },
    "lighting": {
      "style": "soft overcast daylight",
      "notes": "no flash, balanced exposure, preserve stone texture and bouquet color without oversaturation"
    },
    "color_grade": {
      "palette": "muted neutrals brown with a pop of pink flowers",
      "contrast": "gentle documentary contrast",
      "tone": "warm, quiet, romantic"
    },
    "negative_prompt": "text, logo, watermark, blur, lowres, deformed hands, extra fingers, warped stairs, crooked railing, CGI, cartoon, harsh flash, oversaturated colors, heavy artifacts"
  }
}
```

{

      "task_type": "photoreal_candid_smartphone_festival_snapshot",

      "priority": "highest",

      "environment": "night festival crowd, silhouettes and heads in midground, fireworks filling the upper sky, red and gold bursts with smoke clouds",

## Photorealistic Candid Travel Romance Film Still

```
{
  "generation_request": {
    "meta_data": {
      "tool": "NanoBanana Pro",
      "task_type": "photoreal_candid_travel_romance",

      "orientation": "portrait",

        "count": 2,

      },
      "micro_action": "one flicks water off their hair; the other laughs and instinctively leans closer; a quick look holds",
      "environment_details": {
        "location": "{argument name="location" default="Aegean seaside"} near rocks / shore",
        "background": "sea sparkle, rocks, a few distant people blurred, natural clutter like sandals near towel",
        "signage_rule": "no readable text/logos"
      },
      "lighting": "warm sun rim with soft fill; controlled highlights on wet hair; deep but detailed shadows",

      "mood": "alive, intimate, real summer"
    },

      "studio lighting",
      "beauty retouch",
      "plastic skin",
      "model faces",
      "HDR",
      "AI glow",
      "overly perfect hair",
      "luxury styling",
      "text",
      "logo",
      "watermark"
    ]
  }
}

## Moody Vertical Bedroom Portrait with Constraints

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  “subject”: {
    “description”: “An ultra-realistic vertical photograph of her with long dual-toned hair lying on a bed in a moody atmosphere.”,
    “body”: {
      “physique”: “A slender yet curvy hourglass figure characterized by a very narrow, defined waist and wider hips. The stomach is flat and toned. The skin is flawless and completely free of any tattoos.”,
      “pose”: “Lying on her back on soft bedding, captured from a slightly low angle. Her head rests on a fluffy grey pillow, and she gazes directly at the camera with a pouty expression. Her left arm is bent with the hand near her hair, and her right arm extends forward.”,
“features”: “Long straight hair featuring a mix of {argument name="hair color 1" default="platinum blonde"} and {argument name="hair color 2" default="dark brown"} streaks. Striking blue-grey eyes and full lips.”
    }
  },
  “wardrobe”: {
    “top”: “A white, tight-fitting asymmetric crop top with a single strap design.”,
    “bottom”: “A black string bikini bottom worn high on the hips.”,
    “accessories”: “Small silver hoop earrings and a silver chain bracelet on the right wrist.”
  },
  “pose_action”: “Relaxed lounging pose on a bed, creating an intimate and moody atmosphere.”,
  “scene”: {
    “environment”: “A bedroom setting focused on the bed area.”,
    “elements”: “White bed sheets and pillows surrounding the subject. Several plush toys are scattered nearby, including pink and white stuffed bunnies and a round black cat plush with a white eye patch.”,
    “composition”: “Vertical 9:16 aspect ratio. Close-up shot framing the torso and face.”
  },
“lighting”: {
    “setup”: “Atmospheric, dim ambient lighting dominated by cool purple and blue tones.”,
    “details”: “The colored light washes over the scene, highlighting the contours of the body and the textures of the fluffy pillow and hair, creating a distinct moody aesthetic.”
  },
  “camera”: {
    “technical”: “Ultra-realistic, high-resolution photograph. Sharp focus on the face and eyes. The low angle emphasizes the body’s curves.”,
    “constraints”: [
      “Aspect Ratio 9:16”,
      “Subject must have NO TATTOOS (skin must be completely clear)”,
      “Maintain the specific hourglass physique described”,
      “Replicate the purple/blue mood lighting exactly”,
      “Include the specific plush toys described”
    ]
  }
}
```

{

  “camera”: {

“constraints”: [
      “Aspect Ratio 9:16 (Selfie)”,
      “Subject must be taking the photo herself (Selfie)”,

## Raw Documentary Car Interior Portrait

{
  "image_analysis": {
    "subject_pose_correction": {
      "body_position": "Sitting with legs spread wide apart (manspreading pose) in a casual, unladylike posture.",
      "legs": "Knees bent and angled outward, thighs fully visible, creating a triangular composition.",
      "arms": "Left elbow firmly planted on the center console box, left hand propping up the cheek/chin. Right arm resting loosely and extended over the right knee.",

      "interaction": "The subject is sunk deep into the seat, looking bored or effortlessly cool."
    },
    "framing_and_composition": {
      "camera_angle": "Eye-level or slightly low angle, shot from outside the car through the open passenger door.",
      "field_of_view": "Wide angle (24mm or 35mm) to capture the full body, legs, and the car's interior context.",
      "foreground_elements": "The red metal door frame of the car is visible on the edge, fully sharp and textured (no foreground blur).",
      "focus": "Deep depth of field, edge-to-edge sharpness, hyper-focal distance."
    }
  },
  "revised_prompt": {
    "format": "JSON",
    "style": "Raw, Documentary, Sharp Focus, High Fidelity"
  },
  "final_prompt": {
    "text_to_image_prompt": "A raw, ultra-sharp wide-angle full-body shot of a tanned young woman sitting in the passenger seat of a vintage car with a red door frame. She is sitting in a relaxed, 'manspreading' pose with her knees wide apart. She is wearing a vintage black {argument name="shirt type" default="Van Halen t-shirt"}, a black choker, and white sneakers. The camera captures her through the open car door. Golden hour sunlight hits her messy brown hair from behind. Shot on 35mm film stock, f/11 aperture for deep depth of field, ensuring the foreground red door frame, the woman, and the wooden dashboard are all in crisp focus. Visible skin pores, sensory details, film grain, slight noise, chromatic aberration, high contrast, unedited aesthetic, crystal clear textures.",
    "negative_prompt": "bokeh, blurred background, depth of field, blurry foreground, soft focus, tilt-shift, close up, portrait crop, legs crossed, ladylike sitting, knees together, zoomed in, headshot, studio lighting, airbrushed, smooth skin, plastic, 3d render, cartoon, vignette."
  }
}

## Cinematic Portrait with Fire in Hand

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A hyper-realistic, cinematic portrait of the uploaded man
in a dark void. He is raising his right hand in front of his
chest, palm facing slightly inward and upward. His fingers
are spread apart and gently curled in a relaxed, open-
claw gesture, as if cradling an invisible object. A realistic,
bright {argument name="flame color" default="orange"} flame burns directly in the center of his
palm, with tongues of fire rising through the gaps
between his fingers. The warm glow of the fire highlights
the texture of his hand and casts deep shadows
elsewhere. He is wearing a black graphic t-shirt. High
contrast, dramatic lighting, 8k resolution. Aspect ratio 4:5
```

## Cyberpunk Geisha Macro Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "vibe_title_en": "Synthetic Tradition Portrait",
  "master_prompt": "A hyper-realistic extreme close-up portrait of The Protagonist situated in a chaotic backstage dressing alcove. The subject exhibits a 'deconstructed cyberpunk geisha' aesthetic: crimson geometric paint physically textured on the skin, interspersed with tactile, raised plastic smiley-face decals on the cheeks. A hand, encased in a textured red glitter-mesh glove, presses firmly against the subject's cheekbone, creating realistic skin displacement and tension. The lighting is moody and directional, utilizing a deep magenta gel key light and a cyan rim light to sculpt the face, emphasizing the glossy wetness of the lips and the natural irregularities of the skin pores. Shot on a Hasselblad H6D-100c with a 120mm macro lens to capture the individual fibers of the glove and the moisture in the eyes. The image quality mimics Cinestill 800T film stock with visible fine grain, avoiding plastic smoothness in favor of raw, analog fidelity. Background implies blurred velvet textures and tungsten vanity bulbs.",

## Documentary-Style Figure Skating Photo of Celebrities

> Sinematik, lüks portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Pedro Pascal and Sydney Sweeney performing figure skating together at the Winter Olympics. They are on an Olympic ice rink inside a large arena, surrounded by blurred spectators and soft stadium lights in the background. Both are mid-motion on the ice, synchronized and dynamic, captured in a candid, unposed moment.

Pedro Pascal wears a fitted Olympic figure-skating costume suitable for competition, focused expression, visible breath in the cold air. Sydney Sweeney wears a refined, elegant skating outfit, confident and joyful expression, hair styled for performance but slightly loose from movement. Natural body proportions, realistic skin texture with visible pores, subtle redness from the cold, no smoothing or artificial glow.

The ice surface shows skate marks, reflections, and imperfections. Breath vapor, slight motion blur on limbs, and natural sweat/frost details add realism. Lighting is authentic indoor arena lighting, uneven and practical—no cinematic spotlights, no studio look.

Photography style:
iPhone photography realism, handheld shot, slight framing imperfections, natural exposure, realistic white balance, no HDR look, visible film grain and digital noise, compressed highlights, soft shadows, shallow depth of field from phone portrait mode.

Mood & Vibe:
Authentic, documentary, spontaneous Olympic moment, grounded and human, emotionally real.

Camera & Technical Cues:

iPhone camera look

1× lens (≈26mm equivalent)

Handheld, slight motion shake

Natural arena lighting only

Film grain, mild noise, no skin retouching

Aspect Ratio:
3:4 

Negative Prompt:
plastic skin, beauty filters, airbrushed faces, cinematic lighting, studio lights, dramatic spotlighting, CGI look, hyper-sharp skin, waxy texture, unreal ice reflections, fantasy costumes, overprocessed HDR, glamour photography
```

## Surreal Giant Woman at Shibuya Crossing Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A surreal, hyper-realistic urban scene of a colossal giant blonde Caucasian woman in her 20s towering over Tokyo’s Shibuya Crossing. She has fair glowing skin, soft rounded cheeks, full natural pink lips, a small straight nose, and light blue eyes. Her long blonde hair is loose, slightly windswept, and flowing naturally.
She wears bold, stylish fashion: a fitted modern crop top in a white or metallic tone, an open leather or satin jacket draped stylishly, and high-waisted leather pants or a mini skirt for a confident high-fashion street-style look. Minimal luxury jewelry adds an elegant touch.
She is posed like a giant resting over the city — leaning forward with both elbows planted on the rooftops of two modern glass skyscrapers, hands supporting her chin and jawline, upper body weight resting on the buildings. Her head is slightly tilted downward and her eyes are clearly looking down at the streets below, calmly observing the tiny city beneath her with a confident, stoic, dominant expression. She is not looking at the camera.
Below her is the famous Shibuya scramble crossing with clear white diagonal stripes, tiny pedestrians crossing, miniature buses, taxis, and cars driving on the left side. An elevated silver commuter train with a green stripe passes through the mid-ground. Surrounding buildings are dense with Japanese billboards and signage.
The sky is clear bright blue in mid-day sunlight. Lighting is natural hard sunlight with realistic shadows cast onto buildings and streets. Shot from a high drone angle looking down at the crossing but at eye-level with the giant woman. Hyper-realistic style with seamless integration between the giant subject and the miniature city. Aspect ratio 4:5.
```

Create a 8k hd ultra realistic picture of reference uploaded picture face 100 dont change face of, Looking straight to the cameraThis charming Valentine's Day-themed photo features a joyful baby boy lying on a soft, white faux-fur background scattered with vibrant red rose petals. The infant is dressed in a smart white polo shirt paired with red suspenders, a matching red bowtie, and cuffed blue jeans. He is framed by a bouquet of red roses and daisies to his left and a black letter board to his right that cheekily reads, "SORRY LADIES MY MOM IS MY VALENTINE." ## Valentine's Day Baby Girl Portrait Prompt

Create a 8k hd ultra realistic picture of reference uploaded picture face 100 dont change face of, Looking straight to the cameraThe uploaded image is a professionally styled milestone portrait of an infant girl, designed with a romantic Valentine’s Day theme. The baby is lying on her back atop a plush, white faux-fur rug, dressed in a vibrant red ensemble that includes a sequined heart-shaped bodice, a ruffled tulle skirt, and a matching bow headband. To her left sits a lush bouquet of red roses, with individual petals scattered artistically across the rug to add depth and texture. ## Late-Night Coastal Portrait with Flash and Sunset Glow

```
Create a beachy look of me like I'm on a beach where it's dark but someone took my picture with a flash, making my face sun-kissed, slightly glittery, and glowing. In the background, add a sunset where the sun has 98% set, leaving a deep purple-red sky, almost dark. The image is taken from behind my back as I turn toward the camera. I'm wearing a light yellow, loose, half-open linen shirt with sleeves rolled up, paired witha subtle gold seashell necklace. My hair is windswept, giving natural surfer vibes. The picture is cropped from above the waist, focusing on the upper body and glowing beachy aesthetic. 9:16 ratio
```

She has a soft, playful smirk and is looking slightly off-camera. Her dark brown hair is wet and slicked back, suggesting she has just been swimming.
Clothing
She is wearing a classic {argument name="bikini color" default="black"} two-piece bikini. The top features a twist-front bandeau design with thin halter straps, while the bottoms have a delicate ruffled trim along the edges.
Setting & Atmosphere
The scene is set against a backdrop of a vast body of water, likely a lake, with dark green-grey ripples catching the light. The wooden dock beneath her is weathered and damp. The overall mood is relaxed and summery, captured in natural, slightly diffused daylight that highlights the moisture on her skin.
Composition & Style
The composition is a balanced medium shot with a relatively shallow depth of field, keeping the focus sharp on the woman while the water in the background is softly blurred. The color palette is naturalistic and muted, dominated by the deep tones of the water, the warm wood of the dock, and the stark black of her swimwear."

Generate a realistic street photo of a guy on your iPhone in the casual street photography style. Use the face from the uploaded photo as the only and precise appearance standard: preserve facial features, face and body proportions, age, hair color and length at a 1:1 ratio, without even 1% change. The guy is standing on a city street in winter, leaning his shoulder slightly against a black street lamp on the sidewalk. His pose is relaxed, one hand in his pocket, the other hanging loosely. In the background are an intersection, bare winter trees, tall buildings, and a few people in the distance; the asphalt is wet after snow, creating a cool city atmosphere. Clothing: an oversized dark olive hoodie without a hood, a voluminous beige-cream scarf partially covers his head and hangs down slightly; the fabric is wool, the fibers are visible. Light: natural winter daylight, soft shadows, cool white balance, no flash. Realism: ultra-realistic photo, natural skin texture, pores, subtle imperfections, realistic fabric textures (scarf wool, thick cotton hoodie), no neural network effects. Quality: photo from the iPhone's main camera, RAW iPhone look, slight digital noise, natural sharpness. Aspect ratio 3:4.

## Unfiltered Paparazzi-Style Portrait in a Private Jet

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"Ultra-realistic raw DSLR paparazzi-style cinematic portrait of a young woman from the reference image — shot inside a beige leather private jet cabin. Tight waist-up crop, very close camera distance, slightly high-angle perspective, as if captured quickly from the aisle in a candid moment.
The subject leans forward casually, torso angled slightly left, head tilted subtly right.Her expression is unapologetically playful and rebellious: a wide grin with tongue fully out, teeth exposed, cheeks raised, jaw relaxed and loose. Eyes locked directly into the lens through thin oval metal-frame glasses with lightly tinted lenses; eyes in razor-sharp focus while everything else falls off naturally.
Both hands lifted near the chest, elbows bent, palms inward, clearly flipping both middle fingers toward the camera in a spontaneous, unfiltered gesture. Hair appears natural and unstyled, without emphasis on length, color, or shape — no idealization or aesthetic exaggeration.
Skin is fully realistic and unretouched: natural tone variations, visible pores, fine lines, micro-imperfections, slight oil sheen, authentic highlights on the forehead and nose, organic shadow transitions — absolutely no smoothing, beauty filters, or artificial skin effects.
She wears a white graphic T-shirt, a black leather cross-body bag strap hanging naturally across the torso, a silver chain necklace, and a small hoop earring. Tattooed forearms are clearly visible with authentic ink texture, slight skin stretch, and real pigment absorption.
Lighting is harsh natural cabin light from camera-left mixed with ambient overhead lighting, creating slightly uneven exposure, mild blown highlights, gritty contrast, and natural shadowing under the cheekbones and jawline. Background shows blurred beige jet seats and indistinct figures, shallow depth of field with imperfect focus falloff and organic lens blur.
DSLR look, paparazzi candid energy, subtle grain, imperfect framing, real-world color cast, film-like RAW processing, HDR realism, non-AI aesthetic, natural proportions, documentary-style authenticity."
```

## Hyper-Realistic Cinematic Fashion Photography Conversion Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik çizgi roman karesi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Convert the reference manga character into a hyper-realistic live-action image, strictly retaining the character's hairstyle, clothing, expression, facial features, and overall temperament.
The image should use deep depth of field, keeping both the model and the background extremely clear, creating an immersive, cinematic, high-end photography effect.
Style requirement: Cinematic hyper-realistic fashion photography style

High resolution, sharp details like cinematic film
Lighting is a strong dramatic contrast between warm work lights and cool twilight
The model is the main subject, while also showcasing a rich environmental background

Technical specifications:
Film stock: {argument name="film stock" default="Kodak Vision3 500T"}

Suitable for: Cinematic photography, night and low-light scenes, indoor tungsten light environments
Color: Warm and saturated cinematic tones
Grain: Fine film grain texture
Latitude: Excellent shadow detail performance

Lens settings:

Aperture: {argument name="aperture" default="f/8–f/11"} (maintaining deep depth of field, clear across the entire frame)
Resolution: 4K or higher
Depth of field: Deep depth of field, foreground and background are clear

Negative prompts (to avoid):

Blurred background, shallow depth of field, bokeh
Out of focus, facial deformation
Cartoon, anime, CG character, illustration style, painterly texture
Low quality, noise, pixelation
Overexposed strong daylight
```

class ImageGenerator:

        prompt_config = {
            "subject": {
                "actor": "Young African woman",

                "action": "stirring a pot with a wooden spoon over a three-stone fire"
            },

                "fidelity": "Ultra-realistic, high detail",
                "style": self.base_style,

        }
        return prompt_config

# Initialize the generator
session = ImageGenerator()
print(session.generate_village_scene())

## Natural Language Prompt for Miniature World Inside a Teacup

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"Shrink the main subject into a tiny, hyper-realistic miniature person sitting on a small moss-covered island floating inside a teacup filled with tea. The island should look natural with grass, small rocks, and uneven terrain. Add a tiny wooden bridge placed across the tea surface where 2–3 miniature photographers stand, actively taking photos of the subject. Include a tiny wooden rowboat gently floating near the island, creating subtle ripples in the tea.
The teacup should be large, elegant, and floral-designed, placed on a wooden table in a cozy room. Add real-world objects around it like a sugar cube, spoon, and soft window light to emphasize the scale difference. Use cinematic soft daylight coming from a window, creating realistic reflections on the tea surface and shadows from the tiny elements.
The subject’s pose should look natural and thoughtful, interacting with the tiny environment. Add ultra-realistic textures to the tea liquid, porcelain cup, wood table, and moss. Slight depth-of-field blur in the background to focus attention on the miniature world inside the cup.
Make the scene whimsical, surreal, and extremely realistic, designed to make viewers pause and stare to understand the scale illusion. Viral, eye-catching, and highly detailed composition."
```

## Surreal Vinyl Record Mid-Play Visualization

> Retro mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A vinyl record mid-play, its grooves erupting into the music they contain. The needle touches down and sound becomes visible: {argument name="music genre" default="[GENRE/SONG]"} manifests as sculptural waveforms rising from the spinning surface. Musicians emerge in miniature, performing on the label. Different tracks create different landscapes—a jazz section becomes smoky club interior, a crescendo builds into cathedral architecture, a quiet passage forms intimate bedroom scene. Dust motes dance as visible frequencies. The album artwork on the sleeve animates and watches. Worn sleeve edges tell stories of hands that held it. Tube amplifier glows amber in background, speaker cone pulses. The whole room vibrates at 33⅓ RPM. Warm analog tones, vinyl crackle almost visible as golden particulate, 8K, nostalgic hyperrealism.
```

The composition is dense, with eyes, noses, and mouths overlapping and interlocking, creating a sense of disarray and intensity. The lighting is dramatic, casting deep shadows and highlighting the intricate details of each facial feature. The color palette is earthy, dominated by browns, grays, and muted skin tones, with occasional pops of color from {argument name="accent color" default="blue eyes"} or a hint of yellow in some skin. The overall effect is surreal and slightly unsettling, evoking a feeling of fragmented identity or a collective consciousness.
```

## Photorealistic Surreal Scene of a Man Reclining on a Floating Cloud

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A photorealistic, surreal wide shot of a man in the uploaded image reclining comfortably on a floating cloud, high in the atmosphere. The man has a short beard, and wears glasses, a black fitted long-sleeve shirt, olive green chinos, and brown Chelsea boots. He leans back against a grey-and-white striped pillow embedded in the cloud, reading a small book held up in his right hand, with a second open book resting on his chest. The lighting is warm golden-hour sunlight hitting the side of the cloud and subject, contrasting with a clear blue sky above and a dense layer of fluffy white clouds below. Cinematic composition, dreamy atmosphere, 8k resolution. 3:4 aspect ratio.
```

```
Create a cinematic urban street-style portrait using the uploaded image as the only identity reference, preserving the face 100% with no changes to facial features, proportions, skin texture, or expression. A young woman sits casually on a zebra crossing at night, relaxed posture, confident presence. Outfit: black fitted short-sleeve crop top with a small white {argument name="brand logo" default="Nike"} chest logo, high-waisted wide-leg blue-grey denim jeans with light rips, chunky white Nike Air Force sneakers, and a small glossy white {argument name="bag brand" default="GUCCI"} shoulder bag. Long dark wavy hair, center-parted. Black angular cat-eye sunglasses, minimal makeup, matte skin, nude lips. Scene: empty city road with bold white zebra lines, warm streetlights creating cinematic highlights and soft shadows. Camera: slightly elevated angle, street-level perspective, shallow depth of field, photorealistic cinematic lighting, clean composition, no beautification, no face alteration.
```

"(own image standing normally) Full-length, environmental night portrait. Pose & Stance: The model leans casually against the front fascia ofa modern, white compact hatchback car (Hyundai i20). The posture is relaxed. Attire: A white long sleeved top featuring intricate tonal lace appliqué or embroidery detailing on the upper chest yoke. High-waisted, straight-leg denim jeans in a grey wash. Casual blue thong sandals. Environment: A nocturnal roadside setting. The ground is an unpaved, dusty gravel surface. To the left, a rustic building structure with a blue corrugated metal gate is visible. The background right reveals a pitch-black hillside dotted with distant, bokeh city lights. Lighting & Technical: High-contrast mixed artificial lighting. A potent, harsh light source originates from the upper left, creating dramatic, vertical lens flare streaks (light pillars) and casting long, hard shadows of the subject and vehicle to the right. The subject is illuminated by a direct flash or floodlight, creating a stark separation from the dark background."

## Candid night street fashion photograph with film aesthetic

> Afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
High-angle candid night street fashion photograph, shot from an upper floor across the street, looking downward.
A young woman ({argument name="subject identity" default="face photo reference"}) stands alone near a minimarket entrance, slightly off-center. Full body visible with open negative space.
She wears a white T-shirt, a washed blue denim jacket, dark straight-leg jeans, and white canvas sneakers. A small tote bag hangs from one shoulder. Hair loose or tied low, slightly messy.
Bright minimarket lighting spills onto the pavement, creating soft reflections. Glass doors show blurred interior shelves and posters.
She stands still for a moment, holding a white takeaway coffee cup while the other hand adjusts her tote bag strap.
She glances upward casually, unaware of the camera.
Cool fluorescent light mixed with warm street lighting.
Shot on Canon AE-1 film, candid street editorial mood.
Slight desaturation, fine film grain.
Sharp subject, gently softened background.
No other people. No readable text.
Aspect ratio 4:5.
```

{
  "generation_request":{

      "task_type":"cinematic_modern_city_scene_google_safe",

      "priority":"highest"
    },

    "references":{
      "female":"UPLOAD_REF",
      "male":"UPLOAD_REF"
    },

    "output_settings":{
      "aspect_ratio":"4:5",
      "orientation":"portrait",
      "resolution_target":"ultra_high_res",
      "render_style":"ultra_photoreal_cinematic_film_still",
      "film_grain":"subtle_fine_grain",
      "dynamic_range":"natural_not_hdr",
      "color_grade":"warm_golden_hour_soft_skin"
    },

    "anatomy_and_realism":{
      "perfect_anatomy":true,
      "accurate_hands":true,
      "accurate_eyes":true,
      "natural_body_proportions":true,
      "no_extra_fingers":true,
      "no_missing_fingers":true,
      "no_face_warp":true
    },

    "global_constraints":{
      "no_nudity":true,
      "no_suggestive_content":true,
      "no_physical_contact":true,
      "no_text":true,
      "no_logos":true
    },

    "creative_prompt":{
      "scene_summary":"Present-day {argument name="city" default="Istanbul"}, golden hour fading into early evening. A close, warm city moment that feels real.",

      "foreground_story":"They walk extremely close, almost shoulder-to-shoulder but not touching. They slow down for a second as if the city around them disappears. The woman holds a small paper cup near her chest with both hands. The man holds a small paper bag (pastry) slightly lifted as if offering it—without reaching toward her. She looks up at him with a soft, genuine smile; he leans his head slightly closer (no contact) and smiles back. Their eye contact lasts a beat longer than normal, then both look forward again and continue walking. Hands are clearly visible, relaxed, separate.",

      "background_story":"Warm street lamps begin to glow. The Bosphorus is visible at the end of the street with a distant ferry passing. A calm street cat sits on a low wall near them, quietly present. The pavement has subtle golden reflections as if it was lightly cleaned or slightly damp, adding cinematic texture without rain.",

      "environment_details":"Authentic Istanbul textures: worn stone pavement, warm window glow shapes, old facades, soft bokeh from lamps, no readable signs, no modern billboards, few people far behind out of focus so they feel alone in the frame.",

      "wardrobe":"Modern elegant modest style. The woman wears a long coat/trench with a simple outfit in warm neutrals; opaque fabric with realistic folds. The man wears a clean dark coat/jacket and trousers. Both look like real people, not models.",

      "jewelry_hair_nails":{
        "female":{
          "hair":"natural modern styling, soft movement from breeze",
          "nails":"short, clean, natural, no polish, no acrylics",
          "jewelry":"minimal (small studs or none), not flashy"
        },
        "male":{
          "jewelr"
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

"A nostalgic, high-angle outdoor shot features two young women—resmbling Sadie Sink and Millie Bobby Brown—relaxing in a lush green meadow, capturing a 1980s summer aesthetic. One girl with long, wavy red hair and retro sunglasses sits cross-legged on the grass, while the other with short brunette hair rests her head affectionately on her friend's lap, lying back on a vintage striped towel. They are dressed in patterned, high-waisted bikinis with vibrant retro prints. The scene is scattered with era-specific props, including a black portable cassette player and a bicycle wheel visible in the periphery. The lighting is warm and golden, casting soft shadows across the grass, emphasizing a candid, sun-drenched atmosphere of friendship and leisure."

## Modern Bohemian Street Chic Denim Look Prompt

> Görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
General Ambiance and Character Style
Style: The character (same woman at the attached reference images) is wearing an all-denim ensemble that reflects a wonderful mix of "Modern Bohemian" and "Street Chic" styles.

Ambiance: The setting appears to be an event space or the background of an outdoor party. The character is standing at a foosball table, taking a break from the game to look back over her shoulder directly at the camera. The presence of other people and a production crew (a microphone boom can be spotted above) suggests this might be a film or photoshoot set.

Detailed Appearance and Pose
Pose: Standing sideways to the table, the character holds one of the foosball handles. With a slight twist of her body, she delivers a classic "over-the-shoulder" gaze that is both playful and assertive.

Hair and Makeup: Her long, light-brown hair flows naturally over her shoulders. Her makeup is very fresh and "glowy"; the highlighter on her cheekbones particularly shimmers in the daylight.

Outfit: * Top: She is wearing a cropped, long-sleeved denim jacket. The jacket features "Hand of Fatima" (Hamsa) embroideries—a protective symbol in Middle Eastern culture—along with colorful bead details, especially on the shoulder.

Bottom: She wears matching, very form-fitting denim mini-shorts. The back pockets also feature the same Hamsa embroidery and colorful stone embellishments, adding a mystic and artistic flair to the outfit.

Surrounding People and Details
Other Characters: On the opposite side of the table, looking at the main character with a smile, is another woman wearing a beige spaghetti-strap dress (resembling model Chrissy Teigen). To the left, the back of a man in a grey t-shirt—the character’s opponent in the game—is visible.

Table Detail: Resting on the edge of the foosball table is an icy drink in a metallic mug (likely a Moscow Mule cocktail), which reinforces the cheerful and relaxed mood of the setting.
```

## Tropical Sun-Kissed Bikini Lounging Prompt

> Minimal, lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
General Style and Scene Atmosphere
Style: The character  possesses a "Tropical & Sun-Kissed" style that reflects the vibrancy of the summer season.

Ambiance: She is lounging on a stylish wooden sunbed in the garden of a modern white villa. The forest view in the background and the clear blue sky add a fresh and peaceful vacation vibe to the scene.

Appearance and Relaxed Pose
Pose: The character is lying face down on the sunbed, with her legs bent at the knees and raised in the air, her eyes closed with her hands resting on her forehead. 

Face and Hair: Her wet-look platinum blonde hair is slicked back, giving the impression that she has just stepped out of the pool or the sea. She has a very natural makeup look reflecting the sun's glow and a peaceful smile on her face.

Physical Features: The character has a very fit voluptuos hourglass physique and smooth skin. 

Outfit and Accessory Details
Bikini: She wears a vibrant gold bikini set that harmonizes perfectly with her hair color. The thin-string, side-tie design of the bikini reflects minimalist summer fashion.

Accessories: Small gold hoop earrings and a delicate gold chain necklace shimmer under the sunlight, adding an elegant touch of chic to this simple beach look.

Light and Color Palette
Lighting: Direct, bright daylight creates a natural radiance on her skin and wet hair while casting sharp, aesthetic shadows across the scene.

Color Palette: The scene is dominated by an energetic contrast of Blue (sky), Green (nature), and gold (bikini/hair); this color combination symbolizes pure summer joy.

## Hyper-Real Multi-Arm Professional Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
**[USER_PHOTO]** **[{argument name="profession" default="PROFESSION"}]**

Create a hyper-real, studio portrait using [USER_PHOTO] as the exact identity reference (same face, hairstyle, skin texture, and age). Depict the person as a confident [{argument name="profession" default="PROFESSION"}] sitting casually on a modern white ergonomic office chair, legs crossed, centered in frame. Add a stylized “multi-arm” illusion (6–8 arms total) radiating behind the torso like a modern Shiva/Vishnu-inspired silhouette, but photorealistic: each extra arm must have correct anatomy, believable joints, tendons, skin pores, and natural shadowing; no warped fingers.

PROPS (must be profession-relevant): Automatically choose and place different tools that a real [{argument name="profession" default="PROFESSION"}] would use, one per hand/arm, all clearly readable and accurate in design. Examples of categories: primary tool, secondary tool, communication device, measurement/diagnostic tool, accessory, and a “hero” prop. Keep one real smartphone in the main/front hand. Avoid generic random objects—every prop must logically belong to [{argument name="profession" default="PROFESSION"}]. If [{argument name="profession" default="PROFESSION"}] is digital/tech: include items like laptop, tablet, camera/monitor, light meter, stylus, keyboard, mic, etc. If [{argument name="profession" default="PROFESSION"}] is medical: stethoscope, clipboard, otoscope, syringe (safe/clinical), etc. If [{argument name="profession" default="PROFESSION"}] is culinary: chef knife, tongs, plating tweezers, recipe tablet, etc. (Always match [{argument name="profession" default="PROFESSION"}].)

BACKGROUND COLOR (topic-matched): Use a seamless paper studio backdrop with a single dominant color that matches the profession theme (e.g., warm mustard for creative/media, cool teal for healthcare, graphite/steel for engineering, rich olive for outdoors, deep burgundy for luxury). The color should complement skin tones and wardrobe. Add subtle gradient falloff and soft bounce for depth (no flat background).

LIGHTING & SET: High-end commercial studio look. One large soft key light from camera-left, controlled fill from right, crisp rim light to separate subject, and realistic specular highlights on props. Include a large circular silver reflector disk behind the subject (partially visible) for visual punch. Add a subtle shadow on the floor consistent with light direction.

WARDROBE & STYLING: Outfit must be believable for a top-tier [{argument name="profession" default="PROFESSION"}] (clean, modern, slightly stylized), with texture detail (fabric weave, seams). Add minimal accessories (watch/bracelet) that feel premium. Expression: focused, slightly intense, “in the zone.”

COMPOSITION & CAMERA: Full-body vertical portrait (4:5), 50mm lens look, eye-level camera, sharp focus on face, shallow depth of field but props still readable. Ultra-detailed skin, natural color grading, high dynamic range, crisp micro-contrast.

REALISM RULES: No cartoon, no CGI look. No extra faces, no duplicated heads. Hands must be anatomically correct (5 fingers), no melted objects, no unreadable text logos unless naturally present. Keep the scene clean and premium.
```

Cute, innocent young adult blonde woman  sitting comfortably on a neatly made bed, relaxed natural posture with legs gently bent, shoulders soft and at ease. Bright, cozy bedroom interior filled with soft natural light, white and cream color palette, minimal modern decor, neatly arranged pillows, soft linen bedsheets, light wooden bed frame and furniture, a large window nearby with sheer curtains gently diffusing the sunlight. Warm morning or late-afternoon sunlight streaming in, creating soft highlights on her hair and skin, gentle shadows for depth, peaceful and intimate atmosphere, calm lifestyle mood.

Photorealistic, ultra-high detail, hyper-detailed skin texture with visible natural pores and softness, realistic hair strands with depth and volume, accurate fabric textures, lifelike color tones. Shot on Canon DSLR, 50mm f/1.8 lens, shallow depth of field, creamy natural bokeh background, sharp focus on eyes and face, natural lighting only, no artificial flash. Soft cinematic yet true-to-life color grading, lifestyle editorial photography style, clean balanced composition, vertical framing, realistic perspective, professional photography quality.
--ar 3:4 --stylize 250 --v 6 --q 2
```

Create a portrait identical to the attached reference image, preserving the face and facial features 100 percent. A D-portrait shot on Fujifilm Pro 400H film, using an old lens filter. Keep the face and facial structure exactly the same as the reference image. The subject is a young woman wearing a clean white spaghetti-strap dress with a cute, light, flowing design. The bodice has a corset-like or gently gathered fit around the chest and torso, subtly highlighting her beautiful proportions, with soft ruffle or pleated details. Her hairstyle is modern “{argument name="hair style" default="long black butterfly-cut hair"},” or layered hair that frames the face, with lots of volume. The hair is slightly messy, with fine strands partially covering her face. She is leaning by an old wooden window covered with lush green leaves and climbing vines. Sunlight filters softly through the leaves, creating a gentle and beautiful glow. High detail, slight film grain, and natural light passing through the window create a calm, fresh, and relaxing atmosphere. She looks directly at the viewer with a gentle expression. Her right hand holds her hair, with her elbow resting on the window frame. The lighting is soft and natural. The camera angle is from the front, shot from outside the window and slightly tilted to the left. The final image appears bright and hazy from sunlight, with visible film grain texture.
```

## Peaceful Solitude Portrait in Nature Prompt

> Manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a photo of a person based on the reference photo without changing his face and hair. The composition is very calm. Emphasize a feeling of peaceful solitude. Place the focus in the middle of nature. In the middle of a grassy field, a man sits on a black folding chair. It is a camping chair. He looks focused while reading an open novel. He wears the same outfit as in the reference photo. He wears white Asics Gel-1130 shoes, with a small part of white socks visible. He uses white Bluetooth earphones, showing that he is listening to music to help him concentrate. His hair is slightly messy from the wind but still looks neat. His sitting posture is relaxed but upright. He looks fully absorbed in his activity. The photo is taken from a distance using a long shot technique. The photographer appears to shoot from behind a mound of grass or bushes. A large green bokeh effect fills the foreground. Plants in the foreground and a tree branch in the upper right create a natural frame that directs the viewer’s eye to the subject in the center. The human subject looks small compared to the wide green landscape. This highlights a positive sense of solitude and the calmness of the open space. The background is a hill or a sloping meadow with a fresh and even green color. The grass texture looks smooth. In the far upper left, a small part of a path or a lighter ground area is visible. The light is soft and even, like on a slightly cloudy day or in the afternoon. There are no harsh shadows or distracting contrasts. The dominant colors are dark green and light green, which create a calming and refreshing mood. The contrast between the dark outfit and the white earphones makes the subject stand out without breaking the harmony of the natural colors. Aspect ratio 3:4.
```

## Cinematic Sunrise Portrait on a Cliff Edge

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Without altering the man's appearance, this cinematic shot evokes the atmosphere of a quiet sunrise or sunset, with golden light softly enveloping everything around, creating a warm and tranquil palette. The man stands on the edge of a cliff, framed by the hard, rough texture of the stone, lending a sense of cool nature and the grandeur of the mountain landscape, receding into the darkening silhouettes in the background. He is turned in profile to the right, holding a white mug in both hands, from which steam rises—a thin, almost ephemeral cloud of living warmth in the cool surroundings. His dark coat hangs loosely, revealing a textured, chunky knit sweater that accentuates the density and volume of natural fabrics. Deep shadows and warm highlights on the coat add a sense of the dense texture of the wool, and his dark blue or black trousers, slightly clinging to his legs, contrast with his more rugged brown boots, firmly planted on the rocky surface. A gentle breeze barely stirs the hair, adding dynamism to the strong, confident silhouette. The background of dark, brooding, forested mountains fades into the sky, where clouds and soft light create a poetic and slightly melancholy mood, as if time has paused, allowing the subject to pause for a moment, savor the moment, and solitude with nature. Photorealistic image, high texture detail, high quality, VK resolution.  9:16.
```

Raw high-fidelity photograph, iPhone 17 Pro sensor simulation, neutral indoor ambient lighting, zero bokeh, SHARP focus from the foreground subject to the background vanity lights, f/8 aperture for absolute structural depth, subtle digital grain.

Anatomy: Realistic shoulder-joint displacement and upper-body symmetry, reflecting the athletic-to-lean transition characteristic of Image A.
[Environment & Lighting]

Interior Details: Large square porcelain wall tiles in a neutral beige hue with dark grout lines. A polished wooden door frame is in the immediate foreground. In the background reflection, a professional-grade vanity mirror with warm, circular lightbulbs is visible against a neutral wall.

## Cinematic Portrait of Subject Floating High Above Clouds

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
TASK: Use the subject from the uploaded photo as the exact person in the final image.  SUBJECT RULES (STRICT): - Use the uploaded photo as the sole reference for the person. - Preserve the subject’s identity, facial features, body proportions, hairstyle, clothing, and overall likeness. - Do NOT change the subject’s face, age, gender, or identity. - Do NOT stylize the subject into a cartoon or illustration. - The subject must remain fully photorealistic.  SCENE & COMPOSITION: Create a surreal, cinematic photograph of the subject suspended in mid-air, floating as if gently falling, frozen in perfect stillness high above the Earth. The subject’s body appears weightless with a natural falling posture. Clothing and loose elements subtly ripple in the wind.  ENVIRONMENT: - High-altitude sky scene above the cloud layer. - Overcast yet luminous sky with towering storm clouds breaking apart. - Soft, realistic sunlight rays piercing through gaps in the clouds. - Natural volumetric light beams and atmospheric depth. - Subtle cloud vapor and mist drifting around the subject. - A distant commercial passenger airplane appears in the background, small in scale, flying horizontally through the clouds. - The airplane must remain far behind the subject and never overlap or distract from them. - A few birds are also present at varying distances, closer than the airplane, with slight motion blur to reinforce depth and scale.  LIGHTING & COLOR: - Ultra-realistic natural lighting. - Cool blues and soft whites dominate the palette. - Warm golden highlights from sunlight striking cloud edges and parts of the subject. - High dynamic range with realistic contrast.  CAMERA & REALISM: - Cinematic photography style. - Realistic depth of field (no artificial or extreme blur). - Correct perspective and scale consistent with high altitude aviation. - No fantasy lighting, no glowing outlines, no surreal distortions beyond the floating effect.  MOOD & STYLE: - Dramatic yet calm. - Minimalist composition. - Strong sense of altitude, silence, and suspension. - Hyper-realistic weather, clouds, and atmospheric detail. - Looks like a high-end cinematic still, not digital art.  HARD RULES: - Do NOT change the subject’s identity or pose beyond floating. - Do NOT add text, logos, UI, or watermarks. - Do NOT introduce sci-fi or fantasy elements. - Keep everything physically believable and grounded in realism.
```

## Hyper-Realistic Candid Night Race Photo with iPhone Flash Aesthetic

> Fotogerçekçi görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Subject: Person in the image

Create a 3:4 hyper-realistic candid night-time event photo in an unedited iPhone rear-camera flash look. Shot handheld at chest-to-face height, close to the Subject, with a wide-angle feel (≈24–26mm equivalent), slight edge distortion, and deep depth-of-field keeping the Subject, nearby crowd, and racetrack background readable.

Vertical medium-close framing from waist to slightly above the head. The Subject stands near a railing at a racing circuit, body angled slightly sideways, one arm bent casually near the railing, the other relaxed. Framing feels rushed and instinctive—slightly off-center, with background elements overlapping and partially cropped.

Direct on-camera flash fires head-on, strongly illuminating the Subject and creating harsh highlights on skin, hair, and racing jacket, with abrupt shadow falloff behind the shoulders. Stadium lights, LED screens, and signage appear in the background as blown highlights and soft bokeh points. Low-level noise and grain remain visible in darker crowd and structure areas.

Phone-style exposure prioritizes the Subject, lifting highlights and compressing shadows. Mild computational sharpening appears along hair strands, jacket seams, printed logos, and facial contours.

The Subject’s expression is alert and slightly distracted, mouth gently parted, gaze directed off-frame as if reacting to live action nearby. The moment feels mid-event and unposed.

The Subject wears a white-and-dark racing-style jacket with visible motorsport branding and patches over a fitted inner top, showing structured fabric, subtle sheen, and natural folds. A thin necklace catches small flash highlights at the collarbone, with jeans and a belt visible at the waist.

The environment is a crowded night racing circuit with railings, spectators, sponsor signage, LED screens, lighting rigs, and structural beams. The background feels busy, noisy, and energetic, adding visual clutter and motion.

The image should feel like a spontaneous iPhone flash photo taken during a live racing event—chaotic, energetic, and human. No cinematic lighting, no editorial posing, no beauty retouching.

Do not change the Subject’s facial features or proportions. The Subject must look 1000% identical to the reference image.
```

## Cinematic Fashion Editorial in European Old Town at Twilight

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Beautiful young woman with flawless porcelain skin and long, silky, naturally flowing blonde hair, soft strands gently framing her face, eyes peacefully closed with a calm, serene expression, subtle natural makeup emphasizing smooth skin texture, delicate lashes and soft pink lips. She stands gracefully in the middle of a historic European old town street at twilight, surrounded by elegant centuries-old buildings with classic facades, arched windows and warm stone textures. Tram rails run through the street beneath her feet, leading the eye into the distance, adding cinematic depth and perspective.
The scene is captured during golden-blue hour, where the sky blends soft dusky blues with warm amber tones. Traditional street lamps are just beginning to glow, casting a gentle warm light that contrasts beautifully with the cool twilight shadows. The background fades into a soft creamy bokeh of city lights, windows, and distant silhouettes, creating a dreamy, romantic atmosphere.
She is wearing an elegant cream knitted midi dress with a refined, feminine silhouette, softly hugging the body with natural fabric folds and realistic knit texture visible. Draped around her shoulders is a very large pastel pink tartan check scarf with long delicate fringes, slightly oversized, adding softness, movement, and cozy elegance. On her feet are clean white casual sneakers, subtly grounding the look in modern street fashion.
The overall mood is dreamy, romantic, calm, and cinematic, blending timeless European charm with contemporary fashion editorial styling. Lighting is soft yet directional, highlighting realistic skin pores, natural hair shine, and fabric detail. Color grading is cinematic with warm highlights and cool shadows, rich but natural tones, high dynamic range, ultra-sharp focus on the subject with shallow depth of field.
Photorealistic fashion editorial photography, ultra-detailed, high resolution, cinematic composition, natural pose, candid elegance, Vogue-style street fashion shoot, professional lens rendering, realistic textures, smooth tonal transitions, atmospheric depth.
--ar 3:4 --q 2 --stylize 400
```

## High-Fidelity Mirror Selfie with Valentine's Roses and Identity Lock

> Minimal, lüks mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Header] (9:16)
Raw high-fidelity photograph, iPhone 17 Pro sensor simulation, indoor ambient lighting, zero bokeh, SHARP focus from the ornate mirror frame to the background door, f/5.6 aperture for maximum structural depth, authentic digital grain.

[Subject & Identity Locking]
Identity: Absolute preservation of Image A’s specific facial geometry, emphasizing the almond eye shape, high lip volume, and defined chin structure.
Hair Physics: Voluminous dark chocolate textured waves, layered and frizz-free. The hair should exhibit high-definition strand separation, cascading naturally behind the shoulders and framing the visible section of the face.
Bio-Fidelity: Detailed skin physics (micro-pores, satin-finish hydration, fine vellus hair along the jawline). Precise anatomical rendering of the arms and hands, showing realistic skin-tone gradients and a soft-glam makeup aesthetic.
Expression: Magnetic presence; a playful and magnetic half-smile peeking out from behind the smartphone, capturing a high-impact visual allure.
[Outfit & Physicality]
Technical Description: High-impact minimalist evening-wear. A technical strapless compression-fit bodice in matte black.
Textile Geometry: Strapless straight-cut neckline architecture with material-to-skin surface compression.
Accessories: A black smartphone in a technical gold-tone protective case with a decorative star pattern. On the right wrist, a high-end gold quatrefoil-motif (clover) bracelet and a thin gold bangle. Minimalist layered gold necklaces.
Surface Physics: The fabric must exhibit realistic physical tension lines highlighting anatomical bust volume symmetry and upper-torso architecture.
Male Counterpart: A male subject positioned behind her, providing a protective embrace, wearing a black structured outer garment and a white inner layer.
[Pose & Composition]
Geometry: Full-body mirror-reflection framing within a large ornate gold-gilded mirror. The subject is holding a massive bouquet of vibrant pink roses wrapped in white structural paper.
Framing: Vertical 9:16 orientation. The camera is positioned to capture the subject’s reflection, with the ornate gold frame of the mirror acting as a foreground border on the right.
Anatomy: Realistic shoulder-joint displacement and proximal limb geometry. Hand-to-phone contact must show precise finger-joint articulation and skin texture.
[Environment & Lighting]
Atmosphere: Upscale modern domestic interior.
Interior Details: Polished white marble flooring with grey veining; a large dark-wood paneled door in the background; and a minimalist white wall with technical light switches and an intercom unit.
Lighting: Diffused 3500K warm indoor lighting. The ceiling shows realistic caustic light patterns (reflections) from the mirror and polished surfaces. The lighting provides even illumination on the subject’s skin and the saturated pink tones of the roses.
Background Integrity: All backg
```

        "preserve_vibe": true,
        "preserve_palette": true,

      }
    },
